<!-- source-body:V90-B01507 alias=XK9-B01507 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02809 alias=XK9-P02809 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1445] style=Heading1 -->
# 附录A　人类变量接口卡册

<!-- source-body:V90-B01508 alias=XK9-B01508 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02810 alias=XK9-P02810 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1446] style=Normal -->
<pre>本附录完整收录第七部分十一项人类变量（HV01—HV11）的接口卡。每张卡按A—E五区登记：A，身份、命题与适用范围；B，正式依赖与推论边界；C，九轴尺度与对象合同；D，状态、证据与变量流；E，承接、责任、规范、上限与纠错。</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01509 alias=XK9-B01509 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02811 alias=XK9-P02811 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1447] style=Normal -->
<pre>以下11张卡片逐项展开每个变量的全部39个正式字段。依赖项的空集合明确写作“无（空集合）”；它只表示该类依赖没有登记对象，不表示证据充分或经验成立。条件支持路由必须逐条整体读取，不能把不同路由的有利条件事后并取。</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01510 alias=XK9-B01510 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02812 alias=XK9-P02812 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1448] style=Heading2 -->

<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02845 alias=XK9-P02845 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[64]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV01-R0-candidate-object；
claim_level=candidate_description；
when=D0对象合同完整，但尚无符合资格且result_state=supported的G1-instance。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=登记候选人类对象、材料集合、边界争议与G1补证需求。；
result_ceiling=仅到候选对象描述；不得称有限有效结构域，也不得限定HV02-HV11的经验对象范围。
2. route_id=HV01-R1-effective-domain；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=同一对象、尺度、窗口、K与外推单元内的预注册G1-instance取得supported。；
additional_inferential_requires=G1-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记该实例范围内的有限有效结构域、对象识别强度、边界可信度和适用窗。；
result_ceiling=只限预注册SP/T/K与generalization_unit；不得作终极本体、统一意志或授权判断。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02846 alias=XK9-P02846 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[64]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02847 alias=XK9-P02847 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[64]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 只在G1-instance预注册对象、尺度、窗口、结果与外推单位内登记有限有效结构域及识别强度</pre>

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02854 alias=XK9-P02854 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[65]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>九轴尺度画像（scale_profile）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02855 alias=XK9-P02855 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[65]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：关系或事件单元、候选成员总体、边界内外分布及分组规则；X=空间范围：共同场所、组织边界、数字平台与跨域外部环境；T=时间跨度：识别窗口、成员变动周期与边界历史；O=组织层级：角色、团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：原始事件、互动机制、中观关系结构、制度与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始互动、事件序列、成员个案、边界分布、指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接成员、被排除者、间接受影响者、二阶外溢、跨域与代际位置；N=网络拓扑范围：成员关系、边界连接、孤立点与跨域桥接；J=管辖与授权范围：对象命名、边界采用及后续处置分别登记授权；不适用轴须登记not_applicable理由</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02856 alias=XK9-P02856 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[65]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>有效对象（effective_object）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02857 alias=XK9-P02857 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[65]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>由D0声明的候选对象；只有通过预注册G1-instance相对匹配N0的阈值检验后，才在该实例范围登记为有限有效结构域</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02858 alias=XK9-P02858 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[65]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

</td>
</tr>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02861 alias=XK9-P02861 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[65]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

2. 代表性

4. 低可见位置</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02862 alias=XK9-P02862 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[65]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02869 alias=XK9-P02869 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1455] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>D. 状态、证据与变量流</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01521 alias=XK9-B01521 -->
<table data-source-table="V90-T066" data-source-alias="XK9-T066" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]">
<tr data-source-row="1">
<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02870 alias=XK9-P02870 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>字段</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="1,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02871 alias=XK9-P02871 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02872 alias=XK9-P02872 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>状态集合（state）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02873 alias=XK9-P02873 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 候选2. 可识别3. 边界争议4. 不成立</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02874 alias=XK9-P02874 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>可观测项（observables）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02875 alias=XK9-P02875 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 边界内外关系密度与约束差异
2. 成员进入、退出与被排除记录
3. 共同问题、资源通道或制度规则的重复共现
4. 改变分组规则后对象识别是否稳定</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02876 alias=XK9-P02876 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>证据要求（evidence）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02877 alias=XK9-P02877 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. D0候选对象与同一性记录
2. G1-instance预注册表及匹配N0
3. 训练与样本外或外推结果
4. 候选分组与竞争分组的增益比较</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02878 alias=XK9-P02878 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>输入依赖与接口内容（input_dependencies）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02879 alias=XK9-P02879 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. D0只提供候选对象字段，不构成结构成立证据
2. 预注册G1-instance及匹配N0、阈值、模型类、样本或外推单位
3. 观察位置、竞争分组与E1协议</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02880 alias=XK9-P02880 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>输出效应与变量流（output_effects）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02881 alias=XK9-P02881 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 仅在G1-instance通过后限定其余十变量的对象范围；未通过时保持候选或材料集合</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02882 alias=XK9-P02882 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>时间窗与时滞（time_window_and_lag）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02883 alias=XK9-P02883 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>登记识别窗口、边界变动与成员变化时滞</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02884 alias=XK9-P02884 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>不确定性（uncertainty）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02885 alias=XK9-P02885 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>记录边界争议、成员缺席和观察覆盖</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02886 alias=XK9-P02886 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>局部排除区（local_exclusion_zone）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02887 alias=XK9-P02887 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>无法安全表达或未被采样的位置不得被总体代表</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="10">
<td data-source-cell="10,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02888 alias=XK9-P02888 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[10]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>受影响位置（affected_positions）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="10,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02889 alias=XK9-P02889 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[66]/tr[10]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 成员2. 被排除者3. 边界外成本承担者</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01522 alias=XK9-B01522 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02890 alias=XK9-P02890 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1456] style=Normal -->
<!-- empty-paragraph -->

<!-- source-body:V90-B01523 alias=XK9-B01523 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02891 alias=XK9-P02891 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1457] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>E. 承接、责任、规范、上限与纠错</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01524 alias=XK9-B01524 -->
<table data-source-table="V90-T067" data-source-alias="XK9-T067" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]">
<tr data-source-row="1">
<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02892 alias=XK9-P02892 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>字段</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="1,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02893 alias=XK9-P02893 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02894 alias=XK9-P02894 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>承接载体（carrier）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02895 alias=XK9-P02895 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 关系网络2. 组织边界3. 制度记录</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02896 alias=XK9-P02896 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>责任主体（responsible_subject）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02897 alias=XK9-P02897 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 提出结构域判断的分析者
2. 使用该判断的决策者</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02898 alias=XK9-P02898 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>规范地位（normative_status）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02899 alias=XK9-P02899 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>描述性H-World接口，不产生正当性</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02900 alias=XK9-P02900 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>判断上限（judgment_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02901 alias=XK9-P02901 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>只有G1-instance通过时，且仅限预注册对象、尺度、窗口、结果与外推单位，才可登记解释级有限有效对象；否则仅为候选对象或材料集合</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02902 alias=XK9-P02902 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>行动上限（action_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02903 alias=XK9-P02903 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成候选结构域、边界争议与补证需求描述，不授权纳入、排除或处置；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02904 alias=XK9-P02904 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02905 alias=XK9-P02905 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 同一场所中反复共现的人群没有稳定关系或共同约束
2. 分析者划定的群组在改变分组规则后立即消失</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02906 alias=XK9-P02906 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02907 alias=XK9-P02907 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，成员与受影响位置可经安全可达、反报复通道挑战边界、代表性和同一性判据，并触发与原命名或决策链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02908 alias=XK9-P02908 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02909 alias=XK9-P02909 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[67]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内实际撤销结构域登记、移除其对下游对象范围的效力并恢复为材料集合，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01525 alias=XK9-B01525 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02910 alias=XK9-P02910 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1458] style=Normal -->
<!-- empty-paragraph -->

</td>

</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02923 alias=XK9-P02923 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[68]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02925 alias=XK9-P02925 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[68]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02926 alias=XK9-P02926 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[68]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02927 alias=XK9-P02927 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[68]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

<!-- source-body:V90-B01529 alias=XK9-B01529 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02931 alias=XK9-P02931 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1461] style=Normal -->

<!-- source-body:V90-B01530 alias=XK9-B01530 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02932 alias=XK9-P02932 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1462] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>B. 正式依赖与推论边界</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01531 alias=XK9-B01531 -->

<tr data-source-row="6">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02943 alias=XK9-P02943 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[69]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02944 alias=XK9-P02944 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[69]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV02-R0-boundary-inventory；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=成员、资源、信息、权利、责任、跨界接口及正式—实际边界差异可逐项登记。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=描述边界状态、接口通达性、守门位置、拒绝记录与排除风险。；
result_ceiling=只到边界与接口清单；不得断言边界已产生因果选择效应。
2. route_id=HV02-R1-selective-effect；
claim_level=conditional_effect；
when=预注册边界或接口变动经符合资格的G2-instance显示对指定跨界流、准入或拒绝结果有超过阈值的通道效应。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定通道、窗口与位置上的边界选择效应及跨界成本分布。；
result_ceiling=只限已检验通道与结果；不得从空间、组织或影响范围推出J轴管辖与处置权。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02945 alias=XK9-P02945 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[69]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
</tr>

2. 成员身份等于同意
3. 影响范围等于管辖权</pre>

<pre>C. 九轴尺度与对象合同</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01534 alias=XK9-B01534 -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02951 alias=XK9-P02951 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[70]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->

<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02953 alias=XK9-P02953 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[70]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>九轴尺度画像（scale_profile）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02954 alias=XK9-P02954 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[70]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：单次跨界事件、接口使用个案、成员类别总体、准入拒绝分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：物理入口、组织边界、数字接口、司法辖区与跨域通道；T=时间跨度：边界生效期、接口等待、迁移时滞与重组周期；O=组织层级：使用者角色、守门团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：跨界事件、守门互动机制、接口结构、制度规则与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始准入拒绝记录、使用序列、个案、流量分布、服务指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接使用者、被排除者、间接受益或成本位置、二阶外溢、跨域与代际影响；N=网络拓扑范围：接口节点、守门瓶颈、替代路径与跨域连接；J=管辖与授权范围：纳入、排除、接口改变及权利责任调整分别登记授权；升格须记录逐轴差值</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02955 alias=XK9-P02955 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[70]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<pre>对资源、信息、权利或责任流产生选择性的边界</pre>

4. 保护继承</pre>

</tr>

<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02993 alias=XK9-P02993 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>承接载体（carrier）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02994 alias=XK9-P02994 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 成员规则
2. 访问机制
3. 法律或制度边界
4. 技术接口</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02995 alias=XK9-P02995 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02996 alias=XK9-P02996 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P02999 alias=XK9-P02999 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>判断上限（judgment_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03000 alias=XK9-P03000 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>接口与影响证据充分时至诊断级</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03001 alias=XK9-P03001 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>行动上限（action_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03002 alias=XK9-P03002 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成边界状态、接口障碍、排除风险与测试需求描述，不授权改变准入、退出、权利或资源流；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03003 alias=XK9-P03003 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03004 alias=XK9-P03004 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 正式成员边界与实际资源控制边界相反
2. 数字接口开放但物理、语言或安全门槛使部分位置无法进入</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03005 alias=XK9-P03005 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03006 alias=XK9-P03006 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，边界内外受影响者可经安全可达、反报复通道挑战纳入、排除和接口障碍，并触发与原边界决策链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03007 alias=XK9-P03007 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03008 alias=XK9-P03008 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[72]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内纠正成员与拒绝记录、实际恢复受影响的准入权利或接口状态并撤销错误边界行动，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03042 alias=XK9-P03042 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[74]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03043 alias=XK9-P03043 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[74]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV03-R0-candidate-anchor；
claim_level=candidate_description；
when=目标、身份、记忆、承诺、恐惧或共同问题有可追踪表达与承载形式，但尚无符合资格的H1-instance。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=登记候选意义材料、异质表达、代表性争议与H1补证需求。；
result_ceiling=仅称候选意义表达；不得称有效指向锚点或共同意志。
2. route_id=HV03-R1-effective-anchor；
claim_level=conditional_effect；
when=预注册H1-instance在资源配置、行动选择或协调结果中唯一预选的判据取得supported。；
additional_inferential_requires=H1-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记该实例、结果家族、尺度与窗口内的条件性有效指向锚点。；
result_ceiling=不外推到未选资源、行动或协调结果，也不推出真实同意、统一内心或强制统一意义。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03044 alias=XK9-P03044 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[74]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03045 alias=XK9-P03045 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[74]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
</tr>

</tr>

<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03053 alias=XK9-P03053 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[75]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：单次表达或行动、主体个案、候选参与总体、立场分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：关系现场、组织空间、数字公共空间与跨域传播范围；T=时间跨度：表达—行动窗口、承诺周期、漂移与解耦时滞；O=组织层级：行动角色、团队、组织、制度至公共治理生态；C=因果层次：表达事件、意义—行动互动机制、中观协调结构、制度安排与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始表达与行动、事件序列、个案、立场分布、协调指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接参与者、异议者、间接受影响者、二阶协调后果、跨域与代际影响；N=网络拓扑范围：表达传播、协调连接、异质簇群与桥接节点；J=管辖与授权范围：锚点命名、代表性采用、协调或统一要求分别登记授权；必须保留异质锚点</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03054 alias=XK9-P03054 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[75]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>有效对象（effective_object）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03055 alias=XK9-P03055 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[75]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>能改变资源或行动的目标、身份、记忆、承诺、恐惧或共同问题</pre>

</td>
</tr>

2. 异质性
3. 成本收益分布
4. J轴</pre>

</td>
</tr>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03062 alias=XK9-P03062 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[75]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03073 alias=XK9-P03073 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[76]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 预注册意义表达出现前后的资源配置差异
2. 行动选择、协作完成率或冲突模式变化
3. 不同位置对锚点的接受、拒绝与替代表述
4. 比较条件下结果差异是否超过预定阈值</pre>

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03084 alias=XK9-P03084 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[76]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</tr>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03089 alias=XK9-P03089 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1479] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>E. 承接、责任、规范、上限与纠错</pre>

<table data-source-table="V90-T077" data-source-alias="XK9-T077" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]">

</tr>

<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03092 alias=XK9-P03092 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03093 alias=XK9-P03093 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 叙事
2. 承诺
3. 共同记忆
4. 制度目标
5. 问题定义</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03094 alias=XK9-P03094 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03095 alias=XK9-P03095 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 提出代表性主张者2. 据此配置资源者</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03096 alias=XK9-P03096 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>规范地位（normative_status）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03097 alias=XK9-P03097 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>锚点存在不证明其正当</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03098 alias=XK9-P03098 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>判断上限（judgment_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03099 alias=XK9-P03099 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>有行动桥接时至解释级，无桥接时仅描述表达</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03100 alias=XK9-P03100 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>行动上限（action_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03101 alias=XK9-P03101 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成候选锚点、异质表达、比较结果与补证需求描述，不授权统一意义、代表意愿或协调行动；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03102 alias=XK9-P03102 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03103 alias=XK9-P03103 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 反复出现的口号没有改变任何资源配置或行动
2. 高压场景中的一致表达掩盖相互冲突的真实目标</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03104 alias=XK9-P03104 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03105 alias=XK9-P03105 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，成员可经安全可达、反报复通道否认代表性、提交异质目标或拒绝被锚定，并触发与原锚点判断链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03106 alias=XK9-P03106 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03107 alias=XK9-P03107 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[77]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内实际撤销锚点命名、移除其代表性与下游协调效力并恢复异质表达状态，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01557 alias=XK9-B01557 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03108 alias=XK9-P03108 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1480] style=Normal -->
<!-- empty-paragraph -->

<table data-source-table="V90-T078" data-source-alias="XK9-T078" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[78]">

<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03113 alias=XK9-P03113 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[78]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>接口 ID（id）</pre>

<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03142 alias=XK9-P03142 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[79]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV04-R0-generation-typing；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=GC、GS与GE的规定字段可分别登记，并保留无主体涌现与未识别主体状态。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=分别登记候选生成条件、候选生成主体与候选生成事件，不将三者互相替代。；
result_ceiling=只到强类型分类；条件、主体或事件任一存在都不证明另两项或因果生成机制。
2. route_id=HV04-R1-generation-mechanism；
claim_level=mechanism_explanation；
when=预注册G2-instance识别GC、GS或无主体涌现通道对指定GE状态转移的超过阈值效应。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定尺度、窗口和通道内的候选生成机制及其GC、GS、GE分型。；
result_ceiling=不得把条件人格化、把事件倒推为主体，或从生成事实推出正当性、责任与授权。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03143 alias=XK9-P03143 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[79]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<!-- source-body:V90-B01566 alias=XK9-B01566 -->

<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03149 alias=XK9-P03149 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[80]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->

<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03151 alias=XK9-P03151 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[80]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>九轴尺度画像（scale_profile）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03152 alias=XK9-P03152 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[80]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：条件暴露、主体行动与生成事件单元，各自个案总体、分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：生成现场、组织或平台边界、扩散区域与跨域环境；T=时间跨度：条件积累期、主体行动窗、事件时点与扩散时滞；O=组织层级：行动角色、生成团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：生成事件、主体—条件互动机制、中观生成结构、制度安排与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始条件行动事件、生成序列、个案、结果分布、转移指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接生成参与者、承接者、间接受影响者、二阶后果、跨域与代际影响；N=网络拓扑范围：条件传播、主体协作、事件扩散与涌现连接；J=管辖与授权范围：生成识别、启动改变、资源投入及扩散处置分别登记授权；GC、GS、GE分别登记尺度</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03153 alias=XK9-P03153 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[80]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>有效对象（effective_object）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03154 alias=XK9-P03154 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[80]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>形成可检测状态转移的条件、主体与事件组合</pre>

</td>
</tr>

</td>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03158 alias=XK9-P03158 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[80]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 新单位与总体
2. 代表关系
3. 责任类型
4. 外部影响</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03159 alias=XK9-P03159 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[80]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>随尺度改变项（changing_semantics）</pre>

</td>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">

</td>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03170 alias=XK9-P03170 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[81]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 潜在
2. 触发
3. 形成
4. 中断
5. 扩散</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03171 alias=XK9-P03171 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[81]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>可观测项（observables）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03172 alias=XK9-P03172 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[81]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 候选条件出现、改变或移除的时间记录
2. 生成主体的能力、授权、决策与实际行动
3. 生成事件前后预注册状态转移
4. 无主体涌现时局部互动与总体结果的桥接记录</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03173 alias=XK9-P03173 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[81]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>证据要求（evidence）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03174 alias=XK9-P03174 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[81]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 启动记录
2. 条件窗口
3. 主体行动
4. 无主体互动机制</pre>

</td>
</tr>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03200 alias=XK9-P03200 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[82]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成GC、GS、GE候选分型、状态转移描述与补证需求，不授权启动、扩散或停止生成过程；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</tr>

<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03202 alias=XK9-P03202 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[82]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 技术条件被错误描述成具有目标的生成主体
2. 无统一发起者的涌现过程被强行归因给一个可见人物</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03203 alias=XK9-P03203 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[82]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03204 alias=XK9-P03204 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[82]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，被归为生成主体者可经安全可达、反报复通道挑战意图、角色与授权归因，并触发与原分型或决策链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03205 alias=XK9-P03205 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[82]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03206 alias=XK9-P03206 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[82]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内纠正GC、GS、GE类型，实际移除错误意图或责任归因及其下游效力，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=登记当前承接载体、任务、成本、容量、停止权、责任类型与承接缺口。；
result_ceiling=只到当前分型与缺口描述；承接能力不成为义务，CV不成为RS。
2. route_id=HV05-R1-functional-carrier-effect；
claim_level=conditional_effect；
when=符合资格的G2-instance显示指定载体替换、中断、补给或减载对预选功能结果有超过阈值的通道效应。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定载体在已测功能、容量、时延或损耗维度上的候选承接效应。；
result_ceiling=未测维度保持未知；功能效应不得直接生成责任、牺牲义务或资源重配授权。
3. route_id=HV05-R2-intertemporal-reproduction；
claim_level=intertemporal_explanation；
when=当前承接通道已有G2-instance支持，且G3-instance显示其历史变量对后续承接或再生产结果具有条件增量。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance、G3-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定窗口与载体内的跨期承接或再生产候选。；
result_ceiling=不推出历史宿命、不可逆、责任归属或继续承担义务。
4. route_id=HV05-R3-historical-carrier-trace；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=H5-instance对唯一预选的具体载体与持久判据取得supported。；
additional_inferential_requires=H5-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定载体、留痕可观察量与窗口内的持久人类留痕，并向G3-instance提交预先定义的历史变量候选。；
result_ceiling=H5-instance不证明未来路径效应、跨期再生产、修复窗口、责任或行动；这些结论仍须各自的G3、推论与规范程序。</pre>

</td>
</tr>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03250 alias=XK9-P03250 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[85]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03251 alias=XK9-P03251 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[85]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：单项任务或承接事件、载体个案、承接总体、成本容量分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：岗位现场、团队或组织边界、数字系统与跨域服务范围；T=时间跨度：任务周期、维护窗口、恢复时滞与责任有效期；O=组织层级：执行角色、团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：执行事件、任务—资源互动机制、中观承接结构、制度责任安排与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始任务日志、承接序列、载体个案、成本容量分布、绩效指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接承接者、服务依赖者、间接受益或成本位置、二阶外溢、跨域与代际影响；N=网络拓扑范围：承接依赖、替代路径、单点瓶颈与跨域服务网络；J=管辖与授权范围：任务分配、停止、资源调整、归责与补救分别登记授权；CV与RS分别登记尺度</pre>

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03252 alias=XK9-P03252 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[85]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<pre>判断上限（judgment_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03297 alias=XK9-P03297 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>资源与责任链完整时至诊断级</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03298 alias=XK9-P03298 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>行动上限（action_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03299 alias=XK9-P03299 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成CV、RS、成本、容量、停止权与承接缺口描述，以及减载、补资源或重分配需求，不授权任务调整、资源配置、归责或保护；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03300 alias=XK9-P03300 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03301 alias=XK9-P03301 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 最可见的低权限执行者不是决策、授权或受益责任主体
2. 自动化设施承担传导任务但不能承担道德或法律责任</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03302 alias=XK9-P03302 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03303 alias=XK9-P03303 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，承接者可经安全可达、反报复通道挑战任务、资源、成本、停止权受限和归责，并触发与原任务分配或归责链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03304 alias=XK9-P03304 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03305 alias=XK9-P03305 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[87]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内撤销错误任务、资源或归责状态，实际恢复先前任务与记录状态并执行经授权补救，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01589 alias=XK9-B01589 -->

<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03339 alias=XK9-P03339 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[89]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03340 alias=XK9-P03340 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[89]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV06-R0-segment-map；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=至少一个链段的输入、输出、时延、损耗、中断、资源、成本与边界可观察。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=登记局部链段、缺失桥、时滞、损耗、中断点与成本位置。；
result_ceiling=不得由单一链段或动力语言宣称完整链条或有效通道。
2. route_id=HV06-R1-complete-chain-composition；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=指向、生成与承接三个接口记录可在同一对象、尺度、窗口与量的映射中逐段连接。；
additional_inferential_requires=human_variable:HV03、human_variable:HV04、human_variable:HV05；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=登记完整候选动力—承接链及逐段证据覆盖。；
result_ceiling=只到候选链条组成；接口记录齐全不等于各链段具有因果效力。
3. route_id=HV06-R2-effective-channel；
claim_level=mechanism_explanation；
when=完整候选链已组成，且符合资格的G2-instance逐段识别指定通道对目标转移的效应。；
additional_inferential_requires=human_variable:HV03、human_variable:HV04、human_variable:HV05、G2-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记已检验链段和窗口内的有效动力—承接通道、损耗与中断机制。；
result_ceiling=不得从一次贯通推出跨期再生产、责任、正当性或行动授权。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03341 alias=XK9-P03341 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[89]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03344 alias=XK9-P03344 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[89]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

2. 失败归因意愿不足
3. 承接者应自行补洞</pre>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03346 alias=XK9-P03346 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1508] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>C. 九轴尺度与对象合同</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01598 alias=XK9-B01598 -->
<table data-source-table="V90-T090" data-source-alias="XK9-T090" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]">
<tr data-source-row="1">
<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03347 alias=XK9-P03347 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>字段</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="1,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03348 alias=XK9-P03348 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03349 alias=XK9-P03349 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>九轴尺度画像（scale_profile）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03350 alias=XK9-P03350 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：单个链节事件、链条个案、同类链总体、输入输出分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：行动现场、项目或组织边界、数字协作空间与跨域外溢；T=时间跨度：启动、维持、中断、恢复窗口及跨期周期；O=组织层级：发起角色、执行团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：链节事件、动力—资源—任务互动机制、中观承接链、制度安排与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始任务资源记录、链节序列、链条个案、损耗分布、绩效指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接发起与承接位置、间接受益或成本位置、二阶外溢、跨域与代际影响；N=网络拓扑范围：依赖链、替代路径、瓶颈、反馈连接与跨域桥接；J=管辖与授权范围：目标采用、任务分配、资源投入、停止与试验分别登记授权；逐段标明跨轴位置</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03351 alias=XK9-P03351 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>有效对象（effective_object）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03352 alias=XK9-P03352 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>把指向和生成转为持续行动的可追踪链条</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03353 alias=XK9-P03353 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[90]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>跨尺度保持项（scale_invariants）</pre>

</td>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03396 alias=XK9-P03396 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03397 alias=XK9-P03397 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>行动上限（action_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03398 alias=XK9-P03398 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成链条连通、时滞、损耗、中断、成本与承接需求描述，不授权减载、资源调整或试验；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03399 alias=XK9-P03399 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03400 alias=XK9-P03400 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 强烈愿景和集中动员没有持续资源、维护或偿付
2. 表面贯通的链条把关键成本转移给边界外承接者</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03401 alias=XK9-P03401 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03402 alias=XK9-P03402 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，链上承接或受影响位置可经安全可达、反报复通道挑战资源、成本与链条归因，并触发与原链条判断或决策链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03403 alias=XK9-P03403 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03404 alias=XK9-P03404 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[92]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内实际撤销整体链条归因及其下游效力、恢复为节点级描述与未决状态，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03438 alias=XK9-P03438 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[94]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03439 alias=XK9-P03439 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[94]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV07-R0-writeback-classification；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=输入通道、回执、字段前后版本、执行记录、生效时间、持续时间及停止或回滚状态可分别检查。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=区分未提交、已提交、已受理、字段改变、已执行、持续或失效的写回状态。；
result_ceiling=只有字段改变且实际执行才称制度性写回；一次写回不称学习。
2. route_id=HV07-R1-causal-feedback；
claim_level=mechanism_explanation；
when=符合资格的G2-instance显示制度返回通道相对无返回或阻断条件改变预选后续状态或转移。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定字段、通道和窗口内的有效反馈与制度写回效应。；
result_ceiling=不得从反馈存在推出学习、长期修复、正当性或授权扩大。
3. route_id=HV07-R2-feedback-mediated-learning；
claim_level=intertemporal_explanation；
when=有效反馈已有G2-instance支持，且G3-instance显示可保留更新在重复轮次对预定任务提供历史条件增量。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance、G3-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记限定任务、轮次和窗口内的反馈介导学习候选。；
result_ceiling=不得称整体制度已经学习、修复完成或价值方向正确。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03440 alias=XK9-P03440 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[94]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03449 alias=XK9-P03449 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[95]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：单次反馈或申诉、写回个案、反馈总体、受理执行分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：提交渠道、组织或平台边界、制度辖区与跨域申诉范围；T=时间跨度：提交、受理、字段变化、执行、持续与复核时滞；O=组织层级：反馈角色、受理团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：反馈事件、写回互动机制、中观程序结构、制度规则与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始反馈、处理序列、写回个案、结果分布、时效指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接申诉人与承接者、间接受影响者、二阶制度后果、跨域与代际影响；N=网络拓扑范围：反馈通道、受理节点、复核路径、阻塞点与跨层连接；J=管辖与授权范围：受理、字段修改、执行、停止、回滚与补救分别登记授权；登记跨层路径</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03450 alias=XK9-P03450 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[95]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03469 alias=XK9-P03469 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[96]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03497 alias=XK9-P03497 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[97]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成受理、字段变化、执行、持续时间与写回缺口描述，以及复核或程序修复需求，不授权改写记录规则、执行修复或关闭申诉；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03498 alias=XK9-P03498 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[97]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03499 alias=XK9-P03499 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[97]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 申诉获得接收回执但记录、规则、资源和停止条件均未改变
2. 审计报告被发布却没有责任人、时限或后续状态更新</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03500 alias=XK9-P03500 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[97]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03501 alias=XK9-P03501 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[97]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，反馈提交者可经安全可达、反报复通道要求状态、时限、责任人与写回结果，并触发与原受理、写回或决策链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03502 alias=XK9-P03502 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[97]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03503 alias=XK9-P03503 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[97]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内撤销错误更新，实际恢复先前记录、规则、资源、角色或停止条件状态，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03537 alias=XK9-P03537 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[99]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03538 alias=XK9-P03538 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[99]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV08-R0-condition-inventory；
claim_level=candidate_description；
when=资源、规则、位置、安全、指标、平台、AI中介或历史沉积可按位置、尺度和时间窗列出，但尚无符合资格的H4-instance。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=登记候选条件、位置异质性、观察盲区、竞争解释与补证需求。；
result_ceiling=仅称条件清单或候选通道；不得把条件人格化，也不得称权力、中介或反身效应已成立。
2. route_id=HV08-R1-position-or-mediation-effect；
claim_level=conditional_effect；
when=H4-instance在证据覆盖、表达安全或对象行为中唯一预选的成功判据取得supported。；
additional_inferential_requires=H4-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记该实例位置、中介、结果家族和窗口内的遮蔽、放大或行为响应通道。；
result_ceiling=不外推到未选结果家族，不从位置或中介效应推出恶意、责任或自动处置。
3. route_id=HV08-R2-reflexive-response；
claim_level=mechanism_explanation；
when=H4-instance唯一预选反身响应判据，且观测、命名、评分或发布经实际通道到达对象并取得supported。；
additional_inferential_requires=H4-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=E3、CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定观测或发布通道与窗口内的反身响应。；
result_ceiling=一次响应不称持久反身性；不得据此隐藏观察、压制表达或扩大授权。</pre>

</td>
</tr>

<pre>C. 九轴尺度与对象合同</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01630 alias=XK9-B01630 -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03545 alias=XK9-P03545 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03546 alias=XK9-P03546 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03547 alias=XK9-P03547 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>九轴尺度画像（scale_profile）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03548 alias=XK9-P03548 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：单次暴露或评价事件、位置个案、受条件总体、响应分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：互动现场、组织或平台边界、公开数字空间与跨域环境；T=时间跨度：暴露积累、响应、反身变化与消退窗口；O=组织层级：行动与评价角色、团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：暴露事件、条件—行为互动机制、中观权力结构、制度规则与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始暴露表达行为、时间序列、位置个案、响应分布、平台指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接被评价者、间接受影响者、二阶反身后果、跨域与代际影响；N=网络拓扑范围：权力与信息连接、中介节点、遮蔽区、放大路径与跨域传播；J=管辖与授权范围：规则配置、指标使用、公开评价、人工复核与处置分别登记授权；比较位置异质性</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03549 alias=XK9-P03549 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>有效对象（effective_object）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03550 alias=XK9-P03550 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>经实际通道改变行动概率、表达安全或证据分布的条件集合</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03551 alias=XK9-P03551 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

</td>

2. 条件异质性
3. 跨域外部性
4. J轴</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03555 alias=XK9-P03555 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03556 alias=XK9-P03556 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[100]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 关键条件与作用强度可随尺度改变</pre>

</td>
</tr>

</tr>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<pre>D. 状态、证据与变量流</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01633 alias=XK9-B01633 -->
<table data-source-table="V90-T101" data-source-alias="XK9-T101" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]">

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03564 alias=XK9-P03564 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->

<pre>状态集合（state）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03566 alias=XK9-P03566 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 支持
2. 约束
3. 遮蔽
4. 放大
5. 混合</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03567 alias=XK9-P03567 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>可观测项（observables）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03568 alias=XK9-P03568 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 资源、规则、平台或公开条件改变前后的行为差异
2. 不同位置的表达安全、证据覆盖和缺席率
3. 指标、评分或AI中介前后的可见性与处置变化
4. 比较条件下候选通道效应是否超过预定阈值</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03569 alias=XK9-P03569 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03570 alias=XK9-P03570 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

2. 位置差异
3. 平台或指标变化
4. 行为响应</pre>

</td>
</tr>

</tr>

<pre>时间窗与时滞（time_window_and_lag）</pre>

<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

<pre>登记条件积累、响应与消退时滞</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">

<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03578 alias=XK9-P03578 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>记录不可观察条件、共线性和反身变化</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03579 alias=XK9-P03579 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>局部排除区（local_exclusion_zone）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03580 alias=XK9-P03580 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</tr>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03581 alias=XK9-P03581 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[10]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03582 alias=XK9-P03582 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[101]/tr[10]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 优势位置
2. 低权力位置
3. 中介者
4. 被评价者</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01634 alias=XK9-B01634 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03583 alias=XK9-P03583 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1533] style=Normal -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03584 alias=XK9-P03584 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1534] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>E. 承接、责任、规范、上限与纠错</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01636 alias=XK9-B01636 -->
<table data-source-table="V90-T102" data-source-alias="XK9-T102" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]">
<tr data-source-row="1">
<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03585 alias=XK9-P03585 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>字段</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="1,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03586 alias=XK9-P03586 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03587 alias=XK9-P03587 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>承接载体（carrier）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03588 alias=XK9-P03588 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 制度
2. 资源配置
3. 平台
4. 指标
5. 关系网络
6. 历史沉积</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03589 alias=XK9-P03589 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>责任主体（responsible_subject）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03590 alias=XK9-P03590 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 规则制定者
2. 平台运营者
3. 资源配置者
4. 行动决策者</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03591 alias=XK9-P03591 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>规范地位（normative_status）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03592 alias=XK9-P03592 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>条件优势或筛选结果不构成正当性</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03593 alias=XK9-P03593 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>判断上限（judgment_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03594 alias=XK9-P03594 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>机制链与反事实充分时至解释级</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03595 alias=XK9-P03595 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>行动上限（action_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03596 alias=XK9-P03596 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成候选条件通道、位置异质性、证据遮蔽与风险降低需求描述，不授权改变规则平台、资源配置、评价或处置主体；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03597 alias=XK9-P03597 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03598 alias=XK9-P03598 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 相同制度条件下不同安全和权力位置出现相反行动
2. 以权力或平台标签替代实际因果通道后无法解释状态变化</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03599 alias=XK9-P03599 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03600 alias=XK9-P03600 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，不同位置可经安全可达、反报复通道提交机制差异、缺席信号与安全影响，并触发与原势场判断或决策链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03601 alias=XK9-P03601 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03602 alias=XK9-P03602 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[102]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内实际撤销不成立的势场归因、移除位置标签及其下游评价处置效力并恢复未决状态，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01637 alias=XK9-B01637 -->

<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03636 alias=XK9-P03636 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[104]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>条件支持路由（conditional_support_routes）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03637 alias=XK9-P03637 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[104]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. route_id=HV09-R0-instant-task-capacity；
claim_level=descriptive_classification；
when=同一窗口、位置与类型映射下的任务或协调要求、容量、恢复余量及其分布可观察。；
additional_inferential_requires=无（空集合）；
additional_protocol_requires=无（空集合）；
allowed_conclusion=登记瞬时任务—容量关系、余量、积压、局部缺口与恢复状态。；
result_ceiling=只到同窗描述；瞬时峰值或缺口不自动成为过载机制、累积损伤或崩溃。
2. route_id=HV09-R1-overload-mechanism；
claim_level=mechanism_explanation；
when=CM-LOAD的适用条件完整，且符合资格的G2-instance显示负荷、补给、减载或恢复通道对预选结果有超过阈值效应。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance、CM-LOAD；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记指定位置、类型、窗口和通道内的过载或恢复机制候选。；
result_ceiling=不得普遍化为熵、韧性或所有位置必然崩溃，也不直接生成减载或牺牲义务。
3. route_id=HV09-R2-cumulative-overload；
claim_level=intertemporal_explanation；
when=过载机制已有G2-instance与CM-LOAD支持，且G3-instance显示历史负荷对后续容量、错误或恢复具有条件增量。；
additional_inferential_requires=G2-instance、CM-LOAD、G3-instance；
additional_protocol_requires=CAUSAL、E4；
allowed_conclusion=登记预注册载体、窗口和结果内的累积损伤或迟恢复候选。；
result_ceiling=不推出不可逆、必然崩溃、责任归属或具名主体承担义务。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03638 alias=XK9-P03638 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[104]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03639 alias=XK9-P03639 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[104]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03640 alias=XK9-P03640 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[104]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03641 alias=XK9-P03641 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[104]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

2. 高负荷证明奉献
3. 过载主体等于失稳机制</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03643 alias=XK9-P03643 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1541] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>C. 九轴尺度与对象合同</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01646 alias=XK9-B01646 -->
<table data-source-table="V90-T105" data-source-alias="XK9-T105" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]">
<tr data-source-row="1">
<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03644 alias=XK9-P03644 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>字段</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="1,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03645 alias=XK9-P03645 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03646 alias=XK9-P03646 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>九轴尺度画像（scale_profile）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03647 alias=XK9-P03647 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>SP=&lt;A,X,T,O,C,R,I,N,J&gt;；A=聚合层次：单项任务负荷、承接个案、岗位或群体总体、负荷容量分布及聚合规则；X=空间范围：工作或照护现场、组织边界、数字劳动空间与跨域外包范围；T=时间跨度：瞬时峰值、持续积压、恢复时滞、跨期与代际窗口；O=组织层级：承接角色、团队、组织、制度至治理生态；C=因果层次：任务事件、任务—容量互动机制、中观瓶颈结构、制度分配与系统条件；R=观察分辨率：原始任务工时记录、负荷序列、承接个案、容量分布、时延错误指标与摘要，并登记压缩损失；I=影响范围：直接承接者、服务依赖者、间接替代者、二阶外溢、跨域与代际成本；N=网络拓扑范围：任务依赖、关键瓶颈、替代节点、恢复路径与跨域外包网络；J=管辖与授权范围：任务分配、资源调整、停止、绩效使用与补救分别登记授权；保留负荷容量分布</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03648 alias=XK9-P03648 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>有效对象（effective_object）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03649 alias=XK9-P03649 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>给定窗口内任务和协调要求相对可用容量与恢复余量的结构关系</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03650 alias=XK9-P03650 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>跨尺度保持项（scale_invariants）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03651 alias=XK9-P03651 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 负荷、容量、恢复与成本位置</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03652 alias=XK9-P03652 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>升格必补项（required_scale_additions）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03653 alias=XK9-P03653 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 负荷分布
2. 聚合遮蔽
3. 责任继承
4. 代际影响</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03654 alias=XK9-P03654 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>随尺度改变项（changing_semantics）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03655 alias=XK9-P03655 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 瓶颈、容量和恢复方式可随层级改变</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03656 alias=XK9-P03656 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[105]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>不适用对象（non_applicable_objects）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03667 alias=XK9-P03667 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 单位时间任务量、积压、时延和错误率
2. 人员资源容量、隐性劳动与替代可用性
3. 停止、缺席、退出和恢复曲线
4. 平均负荷与关键局部承接位置的分布差异</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03668 alias=XK9-P03668 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>证据要求（evidence）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03669 alias=XK9-P03669 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 任务量
2. 时延与错误
3. 人员与资源
4. 恢复记录
5. 退出和缺席</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03670 alias=XK9-P03670 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>输入依赖与接口内容（input_dependencies）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03671 alias=XK9-P03671 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 承接层
2. 动力—承接链
3. 条件势场
4. R0为同类型、同时间窗、同承担位置的任务量、容量、积压及恢复记录描述，不以G2成立为描述前提。
5. R1若主张指定通道的负荷效应，调用G2与CM-LOAD；R2若主张该机制合同下的累积损伤、迟恢复或历史条件增量，另按原条件调用预注册G3-instance。H5候选留痕不能替代G3。
6. 未满足上述窄机制资格，不取消由独立领域证据支持的一般伤害、持续负担或历史因果判断；两类判断分开命名与登记。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03672 alias=XK9-P03672 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>输出效应与变量流（output_effects）</pre>

</td>

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03673 alias=XK9-P03673 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 状态更新
2. 失稳行为
3. 维护和修复需求</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03674 alias=XK9-P03674 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03675 alias=XK9-P03675 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>区分即时峰值、持续积压、恢复时滞与代际成本</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03676 alias=XK9-P03676 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03677 alias=XK9-P03677 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
</tr>

<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03678 alias=XK9-P03678 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03679 alias=XK9-P03679 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>非正式劳动、家庭照护、外包和低可见承接位置</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="10">
<td data-source-cell="10,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03680 alias=XK9-P03680 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[10]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->

</td>
<td data-source-cell="10,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03681 alias=XK9-P03681 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[106]/tr[10]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 承接者
2. 依赖服务者
3. 替代者
4. 成本外溢位置</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01650 alias=XK9-B01650 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03682 alias=XK9-P03682 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1544] style=Normal -->
<!-- empty-paragraph -->

<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03683 alias=XK9-P03683 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1545] style=CardLabel -->
<pre>E. 承接、责任、规范、上限与纠错</pre>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01652 alias=XK9-B01652 -->
<table data-source-table="V90-T107" data-source-alias="XK9-T107" data-locator="word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]">
<tr data-source-row="1">
<td data-source-cell="1,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03684 alias=XK9-P03684 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[1]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>字段</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="1,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03685 alias=XK9-P03685 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[1]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableHead -->
<pre>登记内容</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="2">
<td data-source-cell="2,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03686 alias=XK9-P03686 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[2]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>承接载体（carrier）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="2,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03687 alias=XK9-P03687 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[2]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 人员
2. 岗位
3. 程序
4. 设施
5. 预算</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="3">
<td data-source-cell="3,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03688 alias=XK9-P03688 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[3]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>责任主体（responsible_subject）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="3,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03689 alias=XK9-P03689 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[3]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 任务分配者
2. 资源配置者
3. 授权者
4. 监督者
5. 补救责任者</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="4">
<td data-source-cell="4,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03690 alias=XK9-P03690 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[4]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>规范地位（normative_status）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="4,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03691 alias=XK9-P03691 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[4]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>高效率或高承载不构成正当性</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="5">
<td data-source-cell="5,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03692 alias=XK9-P03692 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[5]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>判断上限（judgment_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="5,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03693 alias=XK9-P03693 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[5]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>负荷容量和恢复证据充分时至诊断级</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="6">
<td data-source-cell="6,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03694 alias=XK9-P03694 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[6]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>行动上限（action_ceiling）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="6,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03695 alias=XK9-P03695 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[6]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>本变量只生成负荷、容量、恢复、局部过载与减载补资源需求描述，不授权任务削减、资源投入、绩效处置或强迫承担；任何现实调整须另过C12、运行时显式N前提、J授权与O程序</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">
<td data-source-cell="7,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03696 alias=XK9-P03696 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[7]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>反例（counterexamples）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03697 alias=XK9-P03697 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>1. 总体平均容量充足但少数关键承接位置持续过载
2. 只用熵或韧性隐喻却无法识别任务、容量和恢复通道</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
<td data-source-cell="8,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03698 alias=XK9-P03698 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[8]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>申诉（appeal）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="8,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03699 alias=XK9-P03699 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[8]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，承接者可经安全可达、反报复通道报告隐性劳动、过载和恢复需求，并触发与原负荷判断、绩效或任务决策链独立的复核</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="9">
<td data-source-cell="9,1" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03700 alias=XK9-P03700 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[9]/tc[1]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>回滚（rollback）</pre>

</td>
<td data-source-cell="9,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03701 alias=XK9-P03701 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[107]/tr[9]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>依appeal_and_rollback_rule，获准回滚时，由指定责任人在规定时限内撤销错误负荷判断及绩效或责任效力，实际恢复任务、资源与记录状态，保留版本与完成验证</pre>

</td>
</tr>
</table>

<!-- source-body:V90-B01653 alias=XK9-B01653 -->
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03702 alias=XK9-P03702 locator=word/document.xml/body/p[1546] style=Normal -->

</tr>
<tr data-source-row="7">

<td data-source-cell="7,2" data-grid-span="1" data-vmerge="">
<!-- source-paragraph:V90-P03718 alias=XK9-P03718 locator=word/document.xml/body/tbl[108]/tr[7]/tc[2]/p[1] style=TableBody -->
<pre>S0-S6和X0只适用于存在方向、生成主体或事件、承接层与制度化过程的人类意向性集体，且允许跳阶、并行、混合、回退、分裂、合并、休眠、吞并和功能转移。</pre>

</td>
</tr>
<tr data-source-row="8">
